foot (RH) swing f1-f3; both pairs are planted on f0 and f4. Each foot swings for 3 frames with lifts of 2, 3 and 2 px.

the path turns into screen px as offset * f * u, with the lift straight up:

  ne: u = (0.8674, -0.4975), f = 0.8152; a planted foot moves (-2.47, +1.42) px a frame on the canvas
The step is 3.5 px in e (the game's speed), so targets fall on half pixels: round each frame's position so a planted
foot's moves alternate (3 and 4 px in e) and its total stays within 0.5 px of the target. targets.json "views" gives the
sole centre of every labelled foot in each still for every frame; run1\guide_<view>.png shows them (the still faded, then

Method: no image tool, native pixels, as the gorilla walk did it. The body, head, ears, eyes, chest plate and tail are the
still, pixel for pixel, dipping 1 px on f0 and f4 (the frames where a pair lands). The tail is the still's tail moved
whole: it dips 1 px one frame later than the body (f1 and f5), never redrawn, never curled, and never drawn over a leg.
The legs are the still's own legs re-posed. Each leg is hip, upper leg, round steel knee disc, lower leg and steel foot.
The foot and lower leg move together to the target, the knee disc moves about half as far, and the upper leg between hip
and knee is redrawn as a leaning band in the still's own colours and shading bands, stepped 1 px per row or two, with its
lit edge and its dark seam kept. On a lifted foot the lower leg rises with it. Every part is the still's own pixels
TRANSLATED, never rotated or resampled (rotation left dark specks and slivers on the rhino). Every joint and every join
with the body (shoulder, hip, knee) is redrawn by hand: no seams, no straight cut edges, no gaps, no part ending abruptly.
Where a leg slides back, draw the body behind it: no slit may show the background inside the body. Keep each leg's length
within 1 px of the still's. The far legs (darker in the still) are built the same way, one shade darker on their own
ramp, behind the near legs and the body.

def rects(boxes):
 m=np.zeros((192,192),bool)
 for x0,y0,x1,y1 in boxes:m[y0:y1+1,x0:x1+1]=True
 return m

def dark(a):
 a=a.copy()
 # simultaneous palette mapping
 old=a.copy()
 for c,d in [('e0c8ff','a46bff'),('a46bff','6a3cc8'),('6a3cc8','2e1a6e'),('d6d6dc','9d9ba2'),('9d9ba2','3d3a44')]:a[np.all(old==P[c],axis=2)]=P[d]
 return a

def sources(v):
 ref=load(R/f'stills/{v}.png');used=np.zeros((192,192),bool);parts={}
 chest=np.zeros((192,192),bool)
 # Preserve the entire cream chest plate, including its steel highlights and rim.
 if v=='se':
  for yy,lo,hi in [(133,106,117),(134,105,117),(135,105,117),(136,105,117),(137,105,117),(138,106,116),(139,106,116),(140,106,115),(141,107,114),(142,106,113),(143,106,112),(144,107,110),(145,108,110)]:chest[yy,lo:hi+1]=True
 yellow=np.logical_or.reduce([np.all(ref==P[h],axis=2) for h in ['f0d27a','fff4d6']]);chest|=yellow&(X>100)
 native_tail=b.mask(TAIL[v])&(ref[:,:,3]>0)
 used|=native_tail

 for n in ['RF','RH','LF','LH']:
  if n not in D[v]:continue
  q=D[v][n];km=b.mask(q['knee'])&~used&~chest&(ref[:,:,3]>0);lm=b.mask(q['lower'])&~used&~km&~chest&(ref[:,:,3]>0)
  parts[n]={'knee':b.piece(ref,km),'lower':b.piece(ref,lm)};used|=km|lm
 # Hidden legs reconstructed with the same translated native parts, one ramp darker.
 # Their still peeks set depth/foot position. No pasted fragment can become detached.
 hidden={'e':[('LH','RH',6,-7),('LF','RF',8,-6)],'se':[('LH','RH',10,-5)],'ne':[('LF','RF',-12,-5)]}[v]
 for n,fromn,dx,dy in hidden:
  q=dict(D[v][fromn]);q['root']=tuple(np.array(q['root'])+[dx,dy]);q['k']=tuple(np.array(q['k'])+[dx,dy]);q['join']=tuple(np.array(q['join'])+[dx,dy]);q['sole']=tuple(np.array(q['sole'])+[dx,dy]);q['hidden']=True;D[v][n]=q
  parts[n]={p:dark(b.shift(a,dx,dy)) for p,a in parts[fromn].items()}
 if v=='ne':
  # The far hind knee is occluded by the tail in the still, not a tail plate.
  parts['LH']['knee']=dark(b.shift(parts['RH']['knee'],-12,-5))
  D[v]['LH']['k']=(83,136);D[v]['LH']['root']=(83,132)
 # Use the actual visible far sole where there is enough source to recover it.
 if v=='se':
  p=b.piece(ref,rects([(91,142,96,148)]));parts['LH']['lower']=p;D[v]['LH']['sole']=(94,148);D[v]['LH']['join']=(94,142)
 tail=b.piece(ref,native_tail)
 used|=tail[:,:,3]>0;used|=rects(PEEKS[v])&(ref[:,:,3]>0)
 body=b.piece(ref,~used);b.comp(body,b.piece(ref,chest))
 # Remaining native shank outlines are limb paint, not pinned trunk paint.
 # Removing them here prevents the old stationary vertical slivers seen on ape.
 if v=='e':body[(Y>=143)&(X>=100)&(X<=123)]=0
 if v=='se':body[(Y>=146)&(X>=90)&(X<=123)]=0
 if v=='ne':
  body[Y>=147]=0;body[(Y>=133)&(X>=103)&(X<=118)]=0;body[(Y>=139)&(X>=89)&(X<=102)]=0

 # Finish the flank behind the native hip housings. Only body above the leg
 # attachment is filled; the space between shin/foot pairs remains background.
 # Nearest native body band copied by row, as in the gorilla's seam repair.
 fills={'e':[(82,132,96,136),(108,131,117,142)],'se':[(81,127,89,130),(98,133,105,136)],'ne':[(90,135,99,138),(107,124,113,132)]}[v]
 for x0,y0,x1,y1 in fills:
  for y in range(y0,y1+1):
   for x in range(x0,x1+1):
    if body[y,x,3]==0 and ref[y,x,3]:body[y,x]=P['2e1a6e']
 return ref,parts,body,tail

def band(a,p0,p1,width=6,far=False):
 # Native palette bands, stepped by <=1px per row wherever room permits.
 x0,y0=p0;x1,y1=p1;y0,y1=int(y0),int(y1)
 if y1<y0:x0,y0,x1,y1=x1,y1,x0,y0
 for y in range(y0,y1+1):
  t=(y-y0)/max(1,y1-y0);cx=int(np.floor(x0+(x1-x0)*t+.5));l=cx-width//2;r=l+width
  a[y,l:r]=P['2e1a6e'];a[y,l+1:r-1]=P['6a3cc8' if not far else '2e1a6e']
  a[y,l+1:l+3]=P['a46bff' if not far else '6a3cc8'];a[y,r-1]=P['141218']

def make(v):
 for d in ['walk/frames','walk/layers','source']:(R/d).mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
 ref,parts,body,tail=sources(v)
 for label,a in [('body',body),('tail',tail)]:Image.fromarray(a).save(R/f'source/{v}_{label}.png')
 for n,pr in parts.items():
  for p,a in pr.items():Image.fromarray(a).save(R/f'source/{v}_{n}_{p}.png')
 frames=[];rig=[]
 for f in range(8):
  dip=int(f in (0,4));layers={}
  for n,pr in parts.items():
   q=D[v][n];sole=np.array(q['sole'],float);off,lift=T['paths'][n][f];vt=T['views'][v]
   ideal=np.array(vt['visible_feet'][n]['frames'][f]['sole_centre']) if n in vt['visible_feet'] else sole+off*np.array(vt['u'])*vt['f']-[0,lift]
   dx,dy=np.floor(ideal-sole+.5).astype(int);kx=int(np.floor(dx*.5+.5));ky=int(np.floor((dy+dip)*.5+.5))
   a=np.zeros_like(ref);root=np.array(q['root'])+[0,dip];knee=np.array(q['k'])+[kx,ky];join=np.array(q['join'])+[dx,dy]
   band(a,root,knee,width=7 if n.endswith('H') else 6,far=n.startswith('L'))
   # The shin and foot remain one rigid native translation. Draw the small
   # articulated connection UNDER both translated pieces so no butt seam shows.
   band(a,knee,join,width=6,far=n.startswith('L'))
   b.comp(a,b.shift(pr['lower'],dx,dy));b.comp(a,b.shift(pr['knee'],kx,ky))
   bottom=int(sole[1]+dy);a[Y>bottom]=0
   raw=b.shift(pr['lower'],dx,dy);a[(Y>=bottom-1)&(raw[:,:,3]==0)]=0
   layers[n]=a;Image.fromarray(a).save(R/f'walk/layers/{v}_{n}_f{f}.png')
   rig.append(dict(foot=n,frame=f,delta=[int(dx),int(dy)],knee_delta=[kx,ky],source_sole=sole.tolist(),target=ideal.tolist(),lift=lift,hidden_in_still=n not in vt['visible_feet']))
  Image.fromarray(b.shift(tail,0,int(f in (1,5)))).save(R/f'walk/layers/{v}_tail_f{f}.png')
  a=np.zeros_like(ref)
  # Tail always behind legs. Pinned trunk hides far legs only where it really exists.
  b.comp(a,b.shift(tail,0,int(f in (1,5))))
  for n in ['LF','LH']:b.comp(a,layers[n])
  b.comp(a,b.shift(body,0,dip))
  for n in ['RF','RH']:b.comp(a,layers[n])
  if v=='se':
   chestmask=b.mask([(106,133),(117,133),(117,137),(115,141),(110,145),(107,143),(105,138)])
   b.comp(a,b.shift(b.piece(ref,chestmask),0,dip))
  # Above the attachments, pin both paint and transparent background exactly.
  cut={'e':131,'se':127,'ne':124}[v]+dip
  pinned=b.shift(ref,0,dip);tt=b.shift(tail,0,dip);pinned[tt[:,:,3]>0]=0;b.comp(pinned,b.shift(tail,0,int(f in (1,5))))
  a[:cut]=pinned[:cut]
  # Fill only actual enclosed holes: translated native body paint where available,
  # deepest violet in mechanical socket recesses. No blanket silhouette expansion.
  holes=b.fill_holes(a[:,:,3]>0)&(a[:,:,3]==0)
  st=b.shift(ref,0,dip)
  for yy,xx in zip(*np.where(holes)):
   c=tuple(st[yy,xx]);a[yy,xx]=c if c in [P[h] for h in ['e0c8ff','a46bff','6a3cc8','2e1a6e']] else P['2e1a6e']
  # One pass only, as required by WALK_PLAYBOOK section 9. All filled
  # pixels are saved for review; no iterative expansion toward an idle silhouette.
  sl=find_slits(a,ref,dip);done=set();pre=a.copy()
  for xx,yy in sorted(sl,key=lambda p:(p[1],p[0])):
   c=fill_colour(a,ref,dip,xx,yy,done,{P[h][:3] for h in ['e0c8ff','a46bff','6a3cc8','2e1a6e']})
   a[yy,xx]=tuple(c)+(255,) if tuple(c) in {P[h][:3] for h in ['e0c8ff','a46bff','6a3cc8','2e1a6e']} else P['2e1a6e'];done.add((xx,yy))
  holes=b.fill_holes(a[:,:,3]>0)&(a[:,:,3]==0);a[holes]=P['2e1a6e']
  Image.fromarray(pre).save(R/f'walk/layers/{v}_before_seams_f{f}.png')
  Image.fromarray(a).save(R/f'walk/frames/{v}_f{f}.png');frames.append(a)
 old=json.loads((R/'rig.json').read_text()) if (R/'rig.json').exists() else {};old[v]=rig;(R/'rig.json').write_text(json.dumps(old,indent=2))
 boards(v,ref,frames)
 report=R/'REPORT.txt';s=report.read_text() if report.exists() else 'fennec-trot35-run1. Tokens: final total unavailable.\nRF/RH near right; LF/LH far left; supplied labels retained.\n'
 s+=f'{v}: eight native frames drawn; measurements and visual review pending.\n';report.write_text(s)
 print(v,'drawn')

def boards(v,ref,frames):
 sel=b.load_selout();font=ImageFont.truetype('C:/Windows/Fonts/arial.ttf',18);bg=(74,80,76,255)
 board=Image.new('RGBA',(768*9,800),bg);zoom=Image.new('RGBA',(420*9,250),bg);review=Image.new('RGBA',(360*5,400*2),bg)
 for j,a in enumerate([ref]+frames):
  tile=Image.fromarray(sel(a)[0]);board.alpha_composite(tile.resize((768,768),Image.Resampling.NEAREST),(j*768,30));ImageDraw.Draw(board).text((j*768+8,5),v+' '+('STILL' if not j else f'f{j-1}'),font=font,fill='white')
  zoom.alpha_composite(tile.crop((60,118,144,162)).resize((420,220),Image.Resampling.NEAREST),(j*420,25));ImageDraw.Draw(zoom).text((j*420+8,3),v+' '+('STILL' if not j else f'f{j-1}'),font=font,fill='white')
  col,row=(0,0) if j==0 else ((j-1)%4+1,(j-1)//4)
  review.alpha_composite(tile.crop((50,85,140,180)).resize((360,380),Image.Resampling.NEAREST),(col*360,row*400+20));ImageDraw.Draw(review).text((col*360+5,row*400+1),v+' '+('STILL' if not j else f'f{j-1}'),font=font,fill='white')
 board.convert('RGB').save(R/f'{v}_board.png');zoom.convert('RGB').save(R/f'{v}_legs_zoom.png');review.convert('RGB').save(R/f'{v}_review.png')
 for half in range(2):zoom.crop((420*(1+half*4),0,420*(5+half*4),250)).convert('RGB').save(R/f'{v}_legs_half{half}.png')
 pics=[]
 for a in frames:
  tile=Image.new('RGBA',(768,768),bg);tile.alpha_composite(Image.fromarray(sel(a)[0]).resize((768,768),Image.Resampling.NEAREST));pics.append(tile.convert('RGB'))
 pics[0].save(R/f'{v}_preview.gif',save_all=True,append_images=pics[1:],duration=100,loop=0,disposal=2,optimize=False)
 views=[vv for vv in ['e','se','ne'] if (R/f'{vv}_board.png').exists()]
 out=Image.new('RGB',(768*9,800*len(views)),bg[:3]);zz=Image.new('RGB',(420*9,250*len(views)),bg[:3])
 for i,vv in enumerate(views):out.paste(Image.open(R/f'{vv}_board.png'),(0,800*i));zz.paste(Image.open(R/f'{vv}_legs_zoom.png'),(0,250*i))
 out.save(R/'board.png');zz.save(R/'legs_zoom.png')

if __name__=='__main__':
 for v in sys.argv[1:] or ['e','se','ne']:make(v)

# Native pixel helpers reused verbatim from ape_l/run1/build.py.
from pathlib import Path
import ast
from collections import deque
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
B=Path(r"D:/dev/new-mech-rpg/docs/art/moba")
def fill_holes(op):
 seen=op.copy();queue=deque()
 for x in range(192):
  for y in (0,191):
   if not seen[y,x]:seen[y,x]=True;queue.append((y,x))
 for y in range(192):
  for x in (0,191):
   if not seen[y,x]:seen[y,x]=True;queue.append((y,x))
 while queue:
  y,x=queue.popleft()
  for yy,xx in ((y-1,x),(y+1,x),(y,x-1),(y,x+1)):
   if 0<=yy<192 and 0<=xx<192 and not seen[yy,xx]:seen[yy,xx]=True;queue.append((yy,xx))
 return ~seen|op

def mask(poly):
 im=Image.new('1',(192,192));ImageDraw.Draw(im).polygon(poly,fill=1);return np.array(im)

def shift(a,dx=0,dy=0):
 b=np.zeros_like(a);ys,xs=np.where(a[:,:,3]>0);xx=xs+int(dx);yy=ys+int(dy);ok=(xx>=0)&(xx<192)&(yy>=0)&(yy<192);b[yy[ok],xx[ok]]=a[ys[ok],xs[ok]];return b

def comp(a,b):
 m=b[:,:,3]>0;a[m]=b[m]

def piece(a,m):
 b=a.copy();b[~m]=0;return b

def support(a):
 yy,xx=np.where(a[:,:,3]>0);row=int(yy.max());xs=np.where(a[row-1:row+1,:,3].any(axis=0))[0];return np.array([(xs.min()+xs.max())/2,row],float)

def load_selout():
 # touchup_machine imports this exact function; no module top-level code is run.
 sp=B/'rhino_l/take-a/anim/selout_rhino.py'
 tree=ast.parse(sp.read_text());ns={'np':np,'OUTLINE':np.array([20,26,22],np.uint8)}
 exec(compile(ast.Module(body=[n for n in tree.body if isinstance(n,ast.FunctionDef) and n.name=='selout'],type_ignores=[]),str(sp),'exec'),ns)
 return ns['selout']

def pieces(op):
 seen=np.zeros_like(op);out=[]
 for y,x in zip(*np.where(op)):
  if seen[y,x]:continue
  todo=[(int(y),int(x))];seen[y,x]=True;pts=[]
  while todo:
   yy,xx=todo.pop();pts.append((yy,xx))
   for dy in (-1,0,1):
    for dx in (-1,0,1):
     ny,nx=yy+dy,xx+dx
     if 0<=ny<192 and 0<=nx<192 and op[ny,nx] and not seen[ny,nx]:seen[ny,nx]=True;todo.append((ny,nx))
  out.append(pts)
 return sorted(out,key=len,reverse=True)

def locate(a,src):
 # Search the complete native six-row sole stamp, never the target coordinates.
 sole=b.support(src);ys,xs=np.where((src[:,:,3]>0)&(b.Y>=sole[1]-5));cols=src[ys,xs]
 best=None
 for dy in range(-12,13):
  for dx in range(-14,15):
   score=float(np.all(a[ys+dy,xs+dx]==cols,axis=1).mean())
   key=(score,-abs(dx)-abs(dy))
   if best is None or key>best[0]:best=(key,dx,dy)
 (_,score_unused),dx,dy=best
 score=best[0][0]
 # Both support-band endpoints must still exist, with their actual source colours.
 sy=int(sole[1]);by,bx=np.where((src[:,:,3]>0)&(b.Y>=sy-1));left,right=int(bx.min()),int(bx.max())
 ends=[]
 for x in [left,right]:
  ends.append(any(np.array_equal(a[y+dy,x+dx],src[y,x]) for y in [sy-1,sy] if src[y,x,3]>0))
 return sole+[dx,dy],score,[dx,dy],all(ends)

# Walk playbook (walk lane)

How to make a machine's walk at 35 degrees, check it, and price it. Started 2026-11-05 by the walk lane ("Build mech
walk animations", Preston: "Get a walking animations session going that is skilled up from everything we learned").
Update this file when a lesson changes. The run log is `LOG.md` in this folder.

**Sources.** Read this session: `ANIMATION_PLAYBOOK.md` (all), `cam35_fleet/HANDOFF.md`, the rhino 35-degree walk
(`rhino_l/take-a/anim/walk35/`: REPORT.txt, clean/REPORT.txt, brief_run1.txt), the wolf walk4 run
(`wolf_m/take-b/anim/codex-walk4/REPORT.txt`, brief.txt, `common.txt`), the gorilla's set A walk report, the kits
(`docs/plan/moba-kits.md`), and the game feel lane's answers on speed (2026-11-05). General knowledge, not read
this session: the four key poses (Richard Williams, *The Animator's Survival Kit*), animal gait sequences
(Muybridge's plates, Hildebrand's gait studies), and the walk-to-run Froude rule (R. McNeill Alexander). Those
parts are marked as craft proposals. Nothing here is canon until Preston says so.

## 1. Rules that bind every walk
- **Build from the newest views.** Before any frames, look for `<take>/views_cam35_touchup/` and its APPROVED.txt.
  The rhino walk was drawn from `views_cam35` after Preston kept the touch-up, and needed `walk35_touchup.py` to
  recolour 40 frames afterwards.
- **The walk and the stills share one angle.** A machine's walk is drawn at 35 degrees only once its 35-degree stills
  are approved. Mixed angles pop at every start and stop.
- **Above the legs, every frame is the still**, moved only by the bob. Then idle to walk cannot pop. The rhino's
  35-degree walk matched its still on 0% differing pixels above the legs.
- **Legs are the still's own pixels, translated, never rotated or resampled.** Rotation left specks that cost a
  78,180-token cleanup on the rhino. Where a leg leans, step its parts 1 px a row.
- **Joints are redrawn, not pasted.** Pasted parts that did not join caused the cutoffs Preston rejected ("Too many
  cutoff etc."). Every joint and every join with the body is drawn in the still's colours and bands.
- **Feet stay planted** (section 7), and the drawn stride is checked against the game's speed (section 8).
- **No curled tails.** Tails lift and swing but stay straight, with the still's thickness and plate count.
- **Palette, canvas, offsets and feet row are the machine's own.** Keep its `palette.json`; place stills on the 192
  canvas at (+16, 160 - feet row).
- **Judge at 4x with the game's sel-out, the approved still in column 1.** He rejects "barely acceptable".
- **Never write into another lane's folders or the approved views.** This lane writes to `walk/<machine>/` only.
- **The session worktree has no `docs/art/moba`.** Write files in the worktree and copy them over with Bash (the
  fleet lane's method). C: was full on 2026-11-05, so the scratchpad was not usable.

## 2. Key poses (craft, proposal)
A walk is a controlled fall caught by each landing foot. Per foot, four poses:

| Pose | What happens | What the body does |
|---|---|---|
| Contact | The swinging foot lands ahead; the other foot of that pair is at the back of its stance | Hold the still's height |
| Down | Weight drops onto the landing foot | The dip: the body sinks 1 px (L can take 2); the shoulders or hips over that foot lead |
| Passing | The swinging foot passes the planted one, at its highest lift | The body returns to still height |
| Up | The planted foot pushes off | Body at still height (rising over it would lift the head off the still; not used yet) |

- On our machines the body only translates by whole pixels; armour does not squash. The rhino's approved bob is a
  1 px dip on f0 and f4 (the frames a fore foot lands).
- Head and tail lag the body by one frame (section 5). The rhino's pose-guided front walk leaned the head toward the
  planted foot, and Preston liked that walk.

## 3. Gaits by body (proposals unless marked approved)
| Machine | Gait | Phase per 8 frames | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| rhino (L) | four-beat lateral walk | LF swings f1-f3, LH f3-f5, RF f5-f7, RH f7-f1; one foot up on even frames, two on odd; lifts 2, 3, 2 | approved, installed at 35 |
| gorilla (L) | knuckle walk, diagonal pairs | right fist + left foot planted f0-f3, the other pair f4-f7; the fists swing as whole forearms | the set A walk (old angle) used diagonal pairs; the kit says "knuckle walking". Real gorillas use a four-beat walk: both are on the guide for Preston |
| wolf (M) | trot, diagonal pairs | pair A (near fore + far hind) planted f0-f3, pair B f4-f7; lifts 2, 4, 4, 2 | approved at the old angle |
| fennec (S) | trot | 10 fps, STRIDE 0.56 m/s in `fennec_kit.gd` | approved at the old angle; the walk is exempt from f0 = still (seated still) |
| hippo (L), pangolin (M) | four-beat walk, like the rhino | as rhino | proposal |
| chameleon (M) | slow diagonal-pair walk with a side-to-side rock | as wolf, longer holds | proposal |
| kangaroo (M) | hop: both hind feet together, tail and gloves trail | take-off f0-f1, air f2-f5, land f6-f7 | proposal; kit: "big hops" |
| squirrel (S) | hop or bound | as kangaroo, shorter air | proposal; kit: "hops and sudden changes of direction" |
| shoebill (M) | two legs: L planted f0-f3, R f4-f7, a body sway toward the planted leg | 8 frames | proposal |
| hummingbird (S) | no walk: hover drift (wing cycle plus a lean into the move) | wing cycle | kit: "hovering"; check with game feel |
| falcon (M) | check the kit and game feel (walk on talons, or a low glide) | | open |

## 4. Weight and timing by size (proposal; the rhino, wolf and fennec numbers are real)
| Size | Frames | ms a frame | Cycle | Dip | Lifts |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| L | 8 | 125 (8 fps), rhino | 1.0 s | 1 px (2 px on a heavy landing, such as the gorilla dropping onto its fists) | 2-3 px |
| M | 8 | 100 (10 fps), wolf | 0.8 s | 1 px | 2-4 px |
| S | 8 | 100 (10 fps), fennec | 0.8 s | 0-1 px | 3-4 px |

Heavier means longer holds on the planted feet, a lower down pose and smaller lifts. Lighter means more air and a
quicker cycle. A cycle length is a placeholder until game feel and Preston set the speed (section 8).

## 5. Overlap and secondary motion
- **Tails:** straight. They trail the body bob by one frame and swing 1-2 px sideways, opposite the hips.
- **Ears, fins, antennae and cables:** trail by one frame, 1 px. Rigid armour plates move with their body part and
  never flex.
- **Gems, lenses and eyes:** carried with their part by translation, never redrawn smaller. Check them on every frame
  (the gorilla's hex gems broke in its hex_shield ne frames).
- **Arms that are legs** (gorilla fists, kangaroo gloves): the whole forearm block moves, and the shoulder join is
  redrawn.

## 6. Loops, frames and views
- **Seamless loop:** f7 to f0 is a normal step. Each foot does exactly one swing per cycle, the planted step is the
  same across the wrap, and the dip pattern repeats. Check it with an onion skin of f7 under f0.
- **Frames:** 8 a cycle, equal timing. Walks are retimed only with Preston's word (the wolf front and back were
  retimed into pairs on f0 and f4).
- **Views:** draw s, se, e, ne and n. The engine mirrors sw, w and nw, and a turn keeps the frame index.
- **Mirror phase (in the game since 2026-11-05, Preston: "Yes, fix it"):** a mirror swaps left and right, so in sw,
  w and nw the foot that is up is the other one. `show_facing()` in `scripts/moba3d/directional_sprite_3d.gd` now
  moves a walking machine's frame to (i + 4) mod 8 (minions (i + 2) mod 4) when it flips into or out of a mirrored
  view, the e-to-w snap included; idle and actions are untouched. Proof: `tools/debug/walk_turn_check.gd`, gorilla
  foot swaps 3 to 0. So every alternating walk (four-beat, diagonal, biped) must keep its two halves symmetric: the
  foot that swings on f(i) swings on its partner's side on f(i + 4), and the dip lands on f0 and f4. A hop has no
  left-right half, so check how a hop looks across that half-loop jump before drawing one. `walk_tools.py mirror`
  checks a gait.

## 7. Foot lock at 35 degrees
- A ground step of d texels along the body shows on screen as d x f x u. At pitch p and view yaw a (e 0, se 45,
  s 90), the screen vector is (cos a, sin a x sin p). At 35: e u (1, 0), f 1.000; se u (0.867, 0.497), f 0.815;
  s u (0, 1), f 0.574; ne and n the same with y negated.
- **Planted step a frame on the canvas = -P x f x u**, where P is the side-view step in px a frame. At P = 3:
  e (-3, 0); se (-2.12, -1.22); ne (-2.12, +1.22); s (0, -1.72); n (0, +1.72).
- **Lock rule:** the k-th planted frame's sole centre minus k planted steps stays within 2 px of the first. The rhino
  passed with e 0.00, se and ne 0.88, s and n 0.88 px.
- **Depth travel shows now.** At 35, a fore-and-back step in s and n shows at 0.574 of its length (0.16 at the old
  angle), so front and back walks need real foot travel, not reordered frames.
- **Toes toward the camera** may draw 2-3 px under the feet row (rhino s and n 163, se and ne 162). The floor hides
  none of it, and walk and idle both end on row 161 with sel-out.
- **The game camera is 30 degrees and the art is drawn at 35** (game feel, 2026-11-05). An s or n planted step drawn
  for 35 plays about 13% long in game: 0.22 px a frame at P = 3. That is under the 2 px lock gate; logged for game
  feel.

## 8. Stride against speed
- Drawn ground speed = P x fps x texel (m). Texel: L 0.020, M 0.018, S 0.016 m.
- Rhino: 3 x 8 x 0.020 = 0.48 m/s, kept by Preston ("Keep 0.48 m/s").
- The game: `move_speed = 3.5` m/s for every machine, a placeholder (`machine_test_player.gd:7`). `stride_speed`
  is one export for all machines (0.48), and `full_speed_walk_rate` is still 1.0, so the feet slide today.
- To plant at 3.5 m/s, a walk drawn at 0.48 m/s plays 7.3 times fast, or the side step must be 21.9 px a frame at
  L and 8 fps (24.3 at M, 27.3 at S). That is a run, not a walk: the gorilla's whole side view is 79 px long.
- **Natural walk speed (Froude, proposal):** v = sqrt(Fr x 9.81 x leg height in m). A comfortable walk is about
  Fr 0.25; animals switch to a run near Fr 0.5. `walk_tools.py stride` prints this per machine from its leg height.
- **Preston picks**, per game feel: lower move_speed, play the walk fast, draw longer strides, or add a run cycle.
  This lane never picks a speed. `stride_speed` needs a per-machine value once a second machine walks.

## 9. The checks on every walk (`walk_tools.py footlock`, plus the eye)
| Check | Pass |
|---|---|
| Foot lock per visible foot, from the frame pixels | spread 2 px or under in x and y |
| Swing feet | lift matches the gait sheet within 1 px; the swing goes forward along u |
| Above the legs | 0% differing pixels against the still after the dip |
| Lowest row per frame | the feet row, or 2-3 rows under it for toes toward the camera, listed |
| Palette, alpha, holes, strays, canvas edge | the still's colours only, binary alpha, one connected machine, no edge contact |
| Seams (`walk_tools.py seams`) | no slit where a limb slid back from the pinned body (solid in the idle, now an enclosed hole). Codex left them on the gorilla in e, se and ne; one `--fix` pass filled them with the body bands. Look first: open space beside a far limb at the silhouette edge is real and stays open, so never run a second pass |
| Loop | f7 to f0 is a normal step (planted step within 1 px of the others) |
| Mirror phase | the same physical foot up across s/sw and n/nw at each frame, or the offset noted |
| Stride | drawn m/s, and the playback rate needed at the current move_speed, reported |
| Eye | board at 4x with sel-out, still in column 1; a dot-grid review video (`make_walk35_review.py`) |
| Game | packed by the animation lane, then game feel's turn and speed test |

The foot-lock tool finds feet by the bottom of each column, so a foot hidden behind the body cannot be measured from a
composite. Codex's own verify measured those from its limb layers; ask for them in the brief.

## 10. Tools (stock-take 2026-11-05)
| Tool | Free or paid | Use here |
|---|---|---|
| `walk/tools/walk_tools.py` (this lane) | free | `stride`, `gait` (sheet, targets, guides, plan video), `footlock`, `mirror` |
| Animation lane scripts (`rhino_l/take-a/anim/`: make_walk35_rhino.py, make_walk35_review.py, walk35_selout.py, walk35_touchup.py; `wolf_m/take-b/anim/foot_lock_test.py`) | free | brief and target builder, review video, sel-out, recolour |
| Codex token cap (`rhino_l/take-a/anim/walk35/codex_cap/run_codex_capped_d.sh`, codex_tokens.py; same arguments as the old `run_codex_capped.sh`, but runs Codex from `D:/dev/_scratch/codex-cwd`, since hollowtide is moving to D:; animation lane, 2026-11-05) | free | kills a Codex run at a token cap; the watcher checks every 20 s, so a run can end about 3k over its cap |
| Aseprite MCP: render_onion_skin, compare_frames, audit_animation, tweens, Lua | free (local) | onion skins for the loop and the passing pose, frame diffs; needs an .aseprite file |
| PixelLab pixelart_workbench (lint, motion, inspect, repair) | free for subscribers (its own note) | lint for orphan pixels and outline gaps on finished frames |
| Godot 4.7.2 headless check and Movie Maker | free | the in-game check and phone video, run by the lanes that own the game |
| Codex walk run (5 views) | paid: Codex tokens on Preston's plan | redraws the joints; the rhino took 274,960 tokens |
| PixelLab animate_with_skeleton_v3 | paid (inside the free daily quota of 20 generations, 3 a view) | fallback for straight s and n walks; held the rhino, lost the fennec |
| Retro Diffusion walking | paid, $0.14 a run | its side and se walks read as walks; ask before each run |
| SpriteCook animate | paid credits | not tried on a machine yet |

## 11. A walk, step by step
1. Confirm the 35-degree stills are approved, and find the newest folder (touch-up first).
2. `walk_tools.py stride` with the machine's leg height and size; agree P and fps with game feel.
3. `walk_tools.py gait` writes `targets.json`, the guides and the plan video for Preston. Free.
4. Price the Codex run (section 12) and wait for Preston's yes in his own words.
5. Codex run 1 (e, se, ne), then run 2 (s, n), each under a token cap, with the rhino's brief_run1 rules: translate,
   never rotate; joints by hand; body pinned.
6. `walk_tools.py footlock` and `seams` on every view, then the board at 4x with sel-out and a 5x zoom of each
   moving joint. The gorilla's cut-off ne limb passed every number and showed only in the zoom. Add to every brief:
   "where a limb slides back, draw the body behind it; no slit may show the background inside the body".
7. Send the frames to the animation lane to pack, to game feel for the turn and speed test, and to the touch-up
   lane for cleanup. One line to the hub.

def rects(boxes):

def dark(a):

 return a

def band(a,p0,p1,width=6,far=False):
 # Native palette bands, stepped by <=1px per row wherever room permits.
 x0,y0=p0;x1,y1=p1;y0,y1=int(y0),int(y1)
 if y1<y0:x0,y0,x1,y1=x1,y1,x0,y0
 for y in range(y0,y1+1):
  t=(y-y0)/max(1,y1-y0);cx=int(np.floor(x0+(x1-x0)*t+.5));l=cx-width//2;r=l+width
  a[y,l:r]=P['2e1a6e'];a[y,l+1:r-1]=P['6a3cc8' if not far else '2e1a6e']
  a[y,l+1:l+3]=P['a46bff' if not far else '6a3cc8'];a[y,r-1]=P['141218']

# Native pixel helpers reused verbatim from ape_l/run1/build.py.
from pathlib import Path
import ast
from collections import deque
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
B=Path(r"D:/dev/new-mech-rpg/docs/art/moba")
def fill_holes(op):
 seen=op.copy();queue=deque()
 for x in range(192):
  for y in (0,191):
   if not seen[y,x]:seen[y,x]=True;queue.append((y,x))
 for y in range(192):
  for x in (0,191):
   if not seen[y,x]:seen[y,x]=True;queue.append((y,x))
 while queue:
  y,x=queue.popleft()
  for yy,xx in ((y-1,x),(y+1,x),(y,x-1),(y,x+1)):
   if 0<=yy<192 and 0<=xx<192 and not seen[yy,xx]:seen[yy,xx]=True;queue.append((yy,xx))
 return ~seen|op

def mask(poly):
 im=Image.new('1',(192,192));ImageDraw.Draw(im).polygon(poly,fill=1);return np.array(im)

def shift(a,dx=0,dy=0):
 b=np.zeros_like(a);ys,xs=np.where(a[:,:,3]>0);xx=xs+int(dx);yy=ys+int(dy);ok=(xx>=0)&(xx<192)&(yy>=0)&(yy<192);b[yy[ok],xx[ok]]=a[ys[ok],xs[ok]];return b

def comp(a,b):
 m=b[:,:,3]>0;a[m]=b[m]

def piece(a,m):
 b=a.copy();b[~m]=0;return b

def support(a):
 yy,xx=np.where(a[:,:,3]>0);row=int(yy.max());xs=np.where(a[row-1:row+1,:,3].any(axis=0))[0];return np.array([(xs.min()+xs.max())/2,row],float)

def load_selout():
 # touchup_machine imports this exact function; no module top-level code is run.
 sp=B/'rhino_l/take-a/anim/selout_rhino.py'
 tree=ast.parse(sp.read_text());ns={'np':np,'OUTLINE':np.array([20,26,22],np.uint8)}
 exec(compile(ast.Module(body=[n for n in tree.body if isinstance(n,ast.FunctionDef) and n.name=='selout'],type_ignores=[]),str(sp),'exec'),ns)
 return ns['selout']

def pieces(op):
 seen=np.zeros_like(op);out=[]
 for y,x in zip(*np.where(op)):
  if seen[y,x]:continue
  todo=[(int(y),int(x))];seen[y,x]=True;pts=[]
  while todo:
   yy,xx=todo.pop();pts.append((yy,xx))
   for dy in (-1,0,1):
    for dx in (-1,0,1):
     ny,nx=yy+dy,xx+dx
     if 0<=ny<192 and 0<=nx<192 and op[ny,nx] and not seen[ny,nx]:seen[ny,nx]=True;todo.append((ny,nx))
  out.append(pts)
 return sorted(out,key=len,reverse=True)

def locate(a,src):
 # Search the complete native six-row sole stamp, never the target coordinates.
 sole=b.support(src);ys,xs=np.where((src[:,:,3]>0)&(b.Y>=sole[1]-5));cols=src[ys,xs]
 best=None
 for dy in range(-12,13):
  for dx in range(-14,15):
   score=float(np.all(a[ys+dy,xs+dx]==cols,axis=1).mean())
   key=(score,-abs(dx)-abs(dy))
   if best is None or key>best[0]:best=(key,dx,dy)
 (_,score_unused),dx,dy=best
 score=best[0][0]
 # Both support-band endpoints must still exist, with their actual source colours.
 sy=int(sole[1]);by,bx=np.where((src[:,:,3]>0)&(b.Y>=sy-1));left,right=int(bx.min()),int(bx.max())
 ends=[]
 for x in [left,right]:
  ends.append(any(np.array_equal(a[y+dy,x+dx],src[y,x]) for y in [sy-1,sy] if src[y,x,3]>0))
 return sole+[dx,dy],score,[dx,dy],all(ends)

# Walk playbook (walk lane)

How to make a machine's walk at 35 degrees, check it, and price it. Started 2026-11-05 by the walk lane ("Build mech
walk animations", Preston: "Get a walking animations session going that is skilled up from everything we learned").
Update this file when a lesson changes. The run log is `LOG.md` in this folder.

**Sources.** Read this session: `ANIMATION_PLAYBOOK.md` (all), `cam35_fleet/HANDOFF.md`, the rhino 35-degree walk
(`rhino_l/take-a/anim/walk35/`: REPORT.txt, clean/REPORT.txt, brief_run1.txt), the wolf walk4 run
(`wolf_m/take-b/anim/codex-walk4/REPORT.txt`, brief.txt, `common.txt`), the gorilla's set A walk report, the kits
(`docs/plan/moba-kits.md`), and the game feel lane's answers on speed (2026-11-05). General knowledge, not read
this session: the four key poses (Richard Williams, *The Animator's Survival Kit*), animal gait sequences
(Muybridge's plates, Hildebrand's gait studies), and the walk-to-run Froude rule (R. McNeill Alexander). Those
parts are marked as craft proposals. Nothing here is canon until Preston says so.

## 1. Rules that bind every walk
- **Build from the newest views.** Before any frames, look for `<take>/views_cam35_touchup/` and its APPROVED.txt.
  The rhino walk was drawn from `views_cam35` after Preston kept the touch-up, and needed `walk35_touchup.py` to
  recolour 40 frames afterwards.
- **The walk and the stills share one angle.** A machine's walk is drawn at 35 degrees only once its 35-degree stills
  are approved. Mixed angles pop at every start and stop.
- **Above the legs, every frame is the still**, moved only by the bob. Then idle to walk cannot pop. The rhino's
  35-degree walk matched its still on 0% differing pixels above the legs.
- **Legs are the still's own pixels, translated, never rotated or resampled.** Rotation left specks that cost a
  78,180-token cleanup on the rhino. Where a leg leans, step its parts 1 px a row.
- **Joints are redrawn, not pasted.** Pasted parts that did not join caused the cutoffs Preston rejected ("Too many
  cutoff etc."). Every joint and every join with the body is drawn in the still's colours and bands.
- **Feet stay planted** (section 7), and the drawn stride is checked against the game's speed (section 8).
- **No curled tails.** Tails lift and swing but stay straight, with the still's thickness and plate count.
- **Palette, canvas, offsets and feet row are the machine's own.** Keep its `palette.json`; place stills on the 192
  canvas at (+16, 160 - feet row).
- **Judge at 4x with the game's sel-out, the approved still in column 1.** He rejects "barely acceptable".
- **Never write into another lane's folders or the approved views.** This lane writes to `walk/<machine>/` only.
- **The session worktree has no `docs/art/moba`.** Write files in the worktree and copy them over with Bash (the
  fleet lane's method). C: was full on 2026-11-05, so the scratchpad was not usable.

## 2. Key poses (craft, proposal)
A walk is a controlled fall caught by each landing foot. Per foot, four poses:

| Pose | What happens | What the body does |
|---|---|---|
| Contact | The swinging foot lands ahead; the other foot of that pair is at the back of its stance | Hold the still's height |
| Down | Weight drops onto the landing foot | The dip: the body sinks 1 px (L can take 2); the shoulders or hips over that foot lead |
| Passing | The swinging foot passes the planted one, at its highest lift | The body returns to still height |
| Up | The planted foot pushes off | Body at still height (rising over it would lift the head off the still; not used yet) |

- On our machines the body only translates by whole pixels; armour does not squash. The rhino's approved bob is a
  1 px dip on f0 and f4 (the frames a fore foot lands).
- Head and tail lag the body by one frame (section 5). The rhino's pose-guided front walk leaned the head toward the
  planted foot, and Preston liked that walk.

## 3. Gaits by body (proposals unless marked approved)
| Machine | Gait | Phase per 8 frames | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| rhino (L) | four-beat lateral walk | LF swings f1-f3, LH f3-f5, RF f5-f7, RH f7-f1; one foot up on even frames, two on odd; lifts 2, 3, 2 | approved, installed at 35 |
| gorilla (L) | knuckle walk, diagonal pairs | right fist + left foot planted f0-f3, the other pair f4-f7; the fists swing as whole forearms | the set A walk (old angle) used diagonal pairs; the kit says "knuckle walking". Real gorillas use a four-beat walk: both are on the guide for Preston |
| wolf (M) | trot, diagonal pairs | pair A (near fore + far hind) planted f0-f3, pair B f4-f7; lifts 2, 4, 4, 2 | approved at the old angle |
| fennec (S) | trot | 10 fps, STRIDE 0.56 m/s in `fennec_kit.gd` | approved at the old angle; the walk is exempt from f0 = still (seated still) |
| hippo (L), pangolin (M) | four-beat walk, like the rhino | as rhino | proposal |
| chameleon (M) | slow diagonal-pair walk with a side-to-side rock | as wolf, longer holds | proposal |
| kangaroo (M) | hop: both hind feet together, tail and gloves trail | take-off f0-f1, air f2-f5, land f6-f7 | proposal; kit: "big hops" |
| squirrel (S) | hop or bound | as kangaroo, shorter air | proposal; kit: "hops and sudden changes of direction" |
| shoebill (M) | two legs: L planted f0-f3, R f4-f7, a body sway toward the planted leg | 8 frames | proposal |
| hummingbird (S) | no walk: hover drift (wing cycle plus a lean into the move) | wing cycle | kit: "hovering"; check with game feel |
| falcon (M) | check the kit and game feel (walk on talons, or a low glide) | | open |

## 4. Weight and timing by size (proposal; the rhino, wolf and fennec numbers are real)
| Size | Frames | ms a frame | Cycle | Dip | Lifts |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| L | 8 | 125 (8 fps), rhino | 1.0 s | 1 px (2 px on a heavy landing, such as the gorilla dropping onto its fists) | 2-3 px |
| M | 8 | 100 (10 fps), wolf | 0.8 s | 1 px | 2-4 px |
| S | 8 | 100 (10 fps), fennec | 0.8 s | 0-1 px | 3-4 px |

Heavier means longer holds on the planted feet, a lower down pose and smaller lifts. Lighter means more air and a
quicker cycle. A cycle length is a placeholder until game feel and Preston set the speed (section 8).

## 5. Overlap and secondary motion
- **Tails:** straight. They trail the body bob by one frame and swing 1-2 px sideways, opposite the hips.
- **Ears, fins, antennae and cables:** trail by one frame, 1 px. Rigid armour plates move with their body part and
  never flex.
- **Gems, lenses and eyes:** carried with their part by translation, never redrawn smaller. Check them on every frame
  (the gorilla's hex gems broke in its hex_shield ne frames).
- **Arms that are legs** (gorilla fists, kangaroo gloves): the whole forearm block moves, and the shoulder join is
  redrawn.

## 6. Loops, frames and views
- **Seamless loop:** f7 to f0 is a normal step. Each foot does exactly one swing per cycle, the planted step is the
  same across the wrap, and the dip pattern repeats. Check it with an onion skin of f7 under f0.
- **Frames:** 8 a cycle, equal timing. Walks are retimed only with Preston's word (the wolf front and back were
  retimed into pairs on f0 and f4).
- **Views:** draw s, se, e, ne and n. The engine mirrors sw, w and nw, and a turn keeps the frame index.
- **Mirror phase (in the game since 2026-11-05, Preston: "Yes, fix it"):** a mirror swaps left and right, so in sw,
  w and nw the foot that is up is the other one. `show_facing()` in `scripts/moba3d/directional_sprite_3d.gd` now
  moves a walking machine's frame to (i + 4) mod 8 (minions (i + 2) mod 4) when it flips into or out of a mirrored
  view, the e-to-w snap included; idle and actions are untouched. Proof: `tools/debug/walk_turn_check.gd`, gorilla
  foot swaps 3 to 0. So every alternating walk (four-beat, diagonal, biped) must keep its two halves symmetric: the
  foot that swings on f(i) swings on its partner's side on f(i + 4), and the dip lands on f0 and f4. A hop has no
  left-right half, so check how a hop looks across that half-loop jump before drawing one. `walk_tools.py mirror`
  checks a gait.

## 7. Foot lock at 35 degrees
- A ground step of d texels along the body shows on screen as d x f x u. At pitch p and view yaw a (e 0, se 45,
  s 90), the screen vector is (cos a, sin a x sin p). At 35: e u (1, 0), f 1.000; se u (0.867, 0.497), f 0.815;
  s u (0, 1), f 0.574; ne and n the same with y negated.
- **Planted step a frame on the canvas = -P x f x u**, where P is the side-view step in px a frame. At P = 3:
  e (-3, 0); se (-2.12, -1.22); ne (-2.12, +1.22); s (0, -1.72); n (0, +1.72).
- **Lock rule:** the k-th planted frame's sole centre minus k planted steps stays within 2 px of the first. The rhino
  passed with e 0.00, se and ne 0.88, s and n 0.88 px.
- **Depth travel shows now.** At 35, a fore-and-back step in s and n shows at 0.574 of its length (0.16 at the old
  angle), so front and back walks need real foot travel, not reordered frames.
- **Toes toward the camera** may draw 2-3 px under the feet row (rhino s and n 163, se and ne 162). The floor hides
  none of it, and walk and idle both end on row 161 with sel-out.
- **The game camera is 30 degrees and the art is drawn at 35** (game feel, 2026-11-05). An s or n planted step drawn
  for 35 plays about 13% long in game: 0.22 px a frame at P = 3. That is under the 2 px lock gate; logged for game
  feel.

## 8. Stride against speed
- Drawn ground speed = P x fps x texel (m). Texel: L 0.020, M 0.018, S 0.016 m.
- Rhino: 3 x 8 x 0.020 = 0.48 m/s, kept by Preston ("Keep 0.48 m/s").
- The game: `move_speed = 3.5` m/s for every machine, a placeholder (`machine_test_player.gd:7`). `stride_speed`
  is one export for all machines (0.48), and `full_speed_walk_rate` is still 1.0, so the feet slide today.
- To plant at 3.5 m/s, a walk drawn at 0.48 m/s plays 7.3 times fast, or the side step must be 21.9 px a frame at
  L and 8 fps (24.3 at M, 27.3 at S). That is a run, not a walk: the gorilla's whole side view is 79 px long.
- **Natural walk speed (Froude, proposal):** v = sqrt(Fr x 9.81 x leg height in m). A comfortable walk is about
  Fr 0.25; animals switch to a run near Fr 0.5. `walk_tools.py stride` prints this per machine from its leg height.
- **Preston picks**, per game feel: lower move_speed, play the walk fast, draw longer strides, or add a run cycle.
  This lane never picks a speed. `stride_speed` needs a per-machine value once a second machine walks.

## 9. The checks on every walk (`walk_tools.py footlock`, plus the eye)
| Check | Pass |
|---|---|
| Foot lock per visible foot, from the frame pixels | spread 2 px or under in x and y |
| Swing feet | lift matches the gait sheet within 1 px; the swing goes forward along u |
| Above the legs | 0% differing pixels against the still after the dip |
| Lowest row per frame | the feet row, or 2-3 rows under it for toes toward the camera, listed |
| Palette, alpha, holes, strays, canvas edge | the still's colours only, binary alpha, one connected machine, no edge contact |
| Seams (`walk_tools.py seams`) | no slit where a limb slid back from the pinned body (solid in the idle, now an enclosed hole). Codex left them on the gorilla in e, se and ne; one `--fix` pass filled them with the body bands. Look first: open space beside a far limb at the silhouette edge is real and stays open, so never run a second pass |
| Loop | f7 to f0 is a normal step (planted step within 1 px of the others) |
| Mirror phase | the same physical foot up across s/sw and n/nw at each frame, or the offset noted |
| Stride | drawn m/s, and the playback rate needed at the current move_speed, reported |
| Eye | board at 4x with sel-out, still in column 1; a dot-grid review video (`make_walk35_review.py`) |
| Game | packed by the animation lane, then game feel's turn and speed test |

The foot-lock tool finds feet by the bottom of each column, so a foot hidden behind the body cannot be measured from a
composite. Codex's own verify measured those from its limb layers; ask for them in the brief.

## 10. Tools (stock-take 2026-11-05)
| Tool | Free or paid | Use here |
|---|---|---|
| `walk/tools/walk_tools.py` (this lane) | free | `stride`, `gait` (sheet, targets, guides, plan video), `footlock`, `mirror` |
| Animation lane scripts (`rhino_l/take-a/anim/`: make_walk35_rhino.py, make_walk35_review.py, walk35_selout.py, walk35_touchup.py; `wolf_m/take-b/anim/foot_lock_test.py`) | free | brief and target builder, review video, sel-out, recolour |
| Codex token cap (`rhino_l/take-a/anim/walk35/codex_cap/run_codex_capped_d.sh`, codex_tokens.py; same arguments as the old `run_codex_capped.sh`, but runs Codex from `D:/dev/_scratch/codex-cwd`, since hollowtide is moving to D:; animation lane, 2026-11-05) | free | kills a Codex run at a token cap; the watcher checks every 20 s, so a run can end about 3k over its cap |
| Aseprite MCP: render_onion_skin, compare_frames, audit_animation, tweens, Lua | free (local) | onion skins for the loop and the passing pose, frame diffs; needs an .aseprite file |
| PixelLab pixelart_workbench (lint, motion, inspect, repair) | free for subscribers (its own note) | lint for orphan pixels and outline gaps on finished frames |
| Godot 4.7.2 headless check and Movie Maker | free | the in-game check and phone video, run by the lanes that own the game |
| Codex walk run (5 views) | paid: Codex tokens on Preston's plan | redraws the joints; the rhino took 274,960 tokens |
| PixelLab animate_with_skeleton_v3 | paid (inside the free daily quota of 20 generations, 3 a view) | fallback for straight s and n walks; held the rhino, lost the fennec |
| Retro Diffusion walking | paid, $0.14 a run | its side and se walks read as walks; ask before each run |
| SpriteCook animate | paid credits | not tried on a machine yet |

## 11. A walk, step by step
1. Confirm the 35-degree stills are approved, and find the newest folder (touch-up first).
2. `walk_tools.py stride` with the machine's leg height and size; agree P and fps with game feel.
3. `walk_tools.py gait` writes `targets.json`, the guides and the plan video for Preston. Free.
4. Price the Codex run (section 12) and wait for Preston's yes in his own words.
5. Codex run 1 (e, se, ne), then run 2 (s, n), each under a token cap, with the rhino's brief_run1 rules: translate,
   never rotate; joints by hand; body pinned.
6. `walk_tools.py footlock` and `seams` on every view, then the board at 4x with sel-out and a 5x zoom of each
   moving joint. The gorilla's cut-off ne limb passed every number and showed only in the zoom. Add to every brief:
   "where a limb slides back, draw the body behind it; no slit may show the background inside the body".
7. Send the frames to the animation lane to pack, to game feel for the turn and speed test, and to the touch-up
   lane for cleanup. One line to the hub.

"""Rhino cam35 touch-up (sprite touch-up lane, Claude, 2026-11-04).

Preston: "Make a session for touching up sprites using a synthesis of all we've learned all our tools etc.
Especially aseprite. Sel-out, Cap ink, and all the other professional terms or tasks they would use."

Input  (read only): ../views_cam35/{s,se,e,ne,n}.png   (approved by Preston, "Thats it looks good")
Ref    (read only): ../views/{...}.png                  (the original approved stills, the craft standard)
Output:             ../views_cam35_touchup/{...}.png    raw, no outline: the packer adds sel-out at pack time
                    ./board.png, ./metrics.json, ./views_selout_light/ (the light-following sel-out option)

The rule: recolour only. Alpha never changes, so canvas, feet row, offsets and silhouette stay exact.
Passes, in order (each counted separately in metrics.json):
  1. specks   cluster cleanup: yellow clusters of 1-2 px take the colour most of their neighbours have
  2. seams    capped ink on lit faces: a yellow pixel darker than both neighbours across it, with both
              neighbours lit (base or highlight), in a line of 3+ (one-pixel breaks closed), becomes the
              darkest colour #141218: a panel line between plates
  2b. rims    bevel rim: highlight kept on a RIM-deep band along each big highlight plate's lit (top left)
              edges, base yellow inside; ends the "whole plate filled with highlight" look
  3. gaps     charcoal frame: a shadow or deep yellow pixel with a dark line (accent or charcoal, 6+ px) on
              its left or above it sits in that plate's cast shadow (light from the top left) and becomes
              charcoal shadow; capped at CAP x the approved view's frame + seam share
Eyes and the ring around them are never touched.
"""

import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

ap.add_argument("take")                      # e.g. fennec_s/take-b

ap.add_argument("--rim", type=int, default=3)
ap.add_argument("--palette", default=None)   # default <take>/cam35/palette.json, else <take>/palette.json
ap.add_argument("--approved", default=None)  # default fixed/ if present, else views/
ap.add_argument("--out", default=None)       # default <take>/views_cam35_touchup (must end in _touchup)
ap.add_argument("--work", default=None)      # default <take>/cam35_touchup (must end in _touchup)
ap.add_argument("--title", default=None)

HEX = {p["hex"].lstrip("#").lower(): tuple(int(p["hex"].lstrip("#")[i:i + 2], 16) for i in (0, 2, 4)) for p in PAL}

- A ground step of d texels along the body shows on screen as d x f x u. At pitch p and view yaw a (e 0, se 45,
  s 90), the screen vector is (cos a, sin a x sin p). At 35: e u (1, 0), f 1.000; se u (0.867, 0.497), f 0.815;
  s u (0, 1), f 0.574; ne and n the same with y negated.
- **Planted step a frame on the canvas = -P x f x u**, where P is the side-view step in px a frame. At P = 3:
  e (-3, 0); se (-2.12, -1.22); ne (-2.12, +1.22); s (0, -1.72); n (0, +1.72).
- **Lock rule:** the k-th planted frame's sole centre minus k planted steps stays within 2 px of the first. The rhino
  passed with e 0.00, se and ne 0.88, s and n 0.88 px.
- **Depth travel shows now.** At 35, a fore-and-back step in s and n shows at 0.574 of its length (0.16 at the old
  angle), so front and back walks need real foot travel, not reordered frames.
- **Toes toward the camera** may draw 2-3 px under the feet row (rhino s and n 163, se and ne 162). The floor hides
  none of it, and walk and idle both end on row 161 with sel-out.
- **The game camera is 30 degrees and the art is drawn at 35** (game feel, 2026-11-05). An s or n planted step drawn
  for 35 plays about 13% long in game: 0.22 px a frame at P = 3. That is under the 2 px lock gate; logged for game
  feel.

## 8. Stride against speed
- Drawn ground speed = P x fps x texel (m). Texel: L 0.020, M 0.018, S 0.016 m.
- Rhino: 3 x 8 x 0.020 = 0.48 m/s, kept by Preston ("Keep 0.48 m/s").
- The game: `move_speed = 3.5` m/s for every machine, a placeholder (`machine_test_player.gd:7`). `stride_speed`
  is one export for all machines (0.48), and `full_speed_walk_rate` is still 1.0, so the feet slide today.
- To plant at 3.5 m/s, a walk drawn at 0.48 m/s plays 7.3 times fast, or the side step must be 21.9 px a frame at
  L and 8 fps (24.3 at M, 27.3 at S). That is a run, not a walk: the gorilla's whole side view is 79 px long.
- **Natural walk speed (Froude, proposal):** v = sqrt(Fr x 9.81 x leg height in m). A comfortable walk is about
  Fr 0.25; animals switch to a run near Fr 0.5. `walk_tools.py stride` prints this per machine from its leg height.
- **Preston picks**, per game feel: lower move_speed, play the walk fast, draw longer strides, or add a run cycle.
  This lane never picks a speed. `stride_speed` needs a per-machine value once a second machine walks.

## 9. The checks on every walk (`walk_tools.py footlock`, plus the eye)
| Check | Pass |
|---|---|
| Foot lock per visible foot, from the frame pixels | spread 2 px or under in x and y |
| Swing feet | lift matches the gait sheet within 1 px; the swing goes forward along u |
| Above the legs | 0% differing pixels against the still after the dip |
| Lowest row per frame | the feet row, or 2-3 rows under it for toes toward the camera, listed |
| Palette, alpha, holes, strays, canvas edge | the still's colours only, binary alpha, one connected machine, no edge contact |
| Seams (`walk_tools.py seams`) | no slit where a limb slid back from the pinned body (solid in the idle, now an enclosed hole). Codex left them on the gorilla in e, se and ne; one `--fix` pass filled them with the body bands. Look first: open space beside a far limb at the silhouette edge is real and stays open, so never run a second pass |
| Loop | f7 to f0 is a normal step (planted step within 1 px of the others) |
| Mirror phase | the same physical foot up across s/sw and n/nw at each frame, or the offset noted |

import sys
sys.dont_write_bytecode=True
from pathlib import Path
import json
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image,ImageDraw,ImageFont
R=Path(__file__).resolve().parent;R1=R.parent/'run1'
sys.path.insert(0,str(R1));import build as old
b=old.b;Y,X=old.Y,old.X;load=old.load
GOLD=['fff4d6','f0d27a','b8923a']
def gold(a):return np.logical_or.reduce([np.all(a[:,:,:3]==list(bytes.fromhex(h)),axis=2)&(a[:,:,3]>0) for h in GOLD])
def save(a,p):Image.fromarray(a).save(R/p)
def report(s):
 p=R/'REPORT.txt';p.write_text(p.read_text()+s+'\\n')
def layers(v,f):return {n:load(R1/f'walk/layers/{v}_{n}_f{f}.png') for n in ['LH','LF','RH','RF']}
def repair_ne():
 ref,parts,body,tail=old.sources('ne');counts=[];changes=[]
 for f in range(8):
  prev=load(R1/f'walk/frames/ne_f{f}.png');ll=layers('ne',f);a=np.zeros_like(ref)
  for n in ['LH','LF']:b.comp(a,ll[n])
  b.comp(a,b.shift(tail,0,int(f in [1,5])));b.comp(a,b.shift(body,0,int(f in [0,4])))
  for n in ['RH','RF']:b.comp(a,ll[n])
  # Only replace the defective tail/upper far-hind area. Preserve run1 joins elsewhere.
  region=(X>=58)&(X<=100)&(Y>=124)&(Y<=149)
  out=prev.copy();out[region]=a[region]
  save(out,f'walk/frames/ne_f{f}.png')
  counts.append(int((np.logical_or.reduce([np.all(out[:,:,:3]==list(bytes.fromhex(h)),axis=2) for h in ['e0c8ff','a46bff']])&(X>=58)&(X<=91)&(Y>=128)&(Y<=149)).sum()))
  changes.append(int(np.any(out"'!=prev,axis=2).sum()))
  for n in ll:save(ll[n],f'"'walk/layers/ne_{n}_f{f}.png')
 print('ne light counts',counts,'changed',changes)
 report('Part 1 ne: far LH/LF, whole native tail, pinned body, near RH/RF; foot layers unchanged.')
 report('ne tail light pixels in x58-91/y128-149 f0-7: '+str(counts)+'; threshold 63 '+('PASS' if min(counts)>=63 else 'FAIL')+'.')
 return counts

def repair_e():
 ref,parts,body,tail=old.sources('e');rig=json.loads((R1/'rig.json').read_text())['e']
 # Recover the fixed chest plate, with a native-colour continuation beneath the shoulder.
 # Its right silhouette, row bands, and seam come from the still.
 plate=np.zeros_like(ref)
 for y,lo,hi in [(129,117,120),(130,115,122),(131,114,123),(132,114,123),(133,114,123),(134,114,123),(135,114,122),(136,114,121),(137,114,121),(138,115,120),(139,115,119),(140,116,118),(141,116,118)]:
  for x in range(lo,hi+1):
   c=ref[y,x]
   if gold(ref)[y,x] or (x>=119 and c[3]):plate[y,x]=c
   else:plate[y,x]=old.P['f0d27a'] if y<138 else tuple(bytes.fromhex('b8923a'))+(255,)
  plate[y,lo-1]=old.P['141218']

def save(a,p):Image.fromarray(a).save(R/p)
def rect(x0,y0,x1,y1):return (X>=x0)&(X<=x1)&(Y>=y0)&(Y<=y1)
def sources(v):
 ref=load(R/f'stills/{v}.png');parts={};used=np.zeros((192,192),bool);tail=np.zeros_like(ref)
 if v=='n':
  # Straight native tail assembly: complete steel plates, violet bands, gold tip.
  tm=rect(89,129,102,149)|rect(92,150,99,150)|rect(94,151,97,151)
  tail=b.piece(ref,tm);used|=tm
 for n,q in D[v].items():
  if 'lower' not in q:continue
  parts[n]={}
  for p in ['knee','lower']:
   m=b.mask(q[p])&~used&(ref[:,:,3]>0)
   # Chest and tail paint belongs to the pinned chest or whole tail, never a leg.
   m &= ~np.logical_or.reduce([np.all(ref[:,:,:3]==list(bytes.fromhex(h)),axis=2) for h in ['fff4d6','f0d27a','b8923a']])
   parts[n][p]=b.piece(ref,m);used|=m
 if v=='s':
  # Hidden hind shins/feet completed from native five-column steel toe blocks.
  # Crop/translate only: the still exposes just the lateral half of each rear foot.
  for n,srcn,x0,x1,dx in [('RH','RF',84,88,-4),('LH','LF',103,107,4)]:
   lower=b.piece(ref,rect(x0,147,x1,160));lower=old.dark(b.shift(lower,dx,-11))
   knee=b.piece(ref,rect(82 if n=='RH' else 104,132,87 if n=='RH' else 109,138));knee=old.dark(b.shift(knee,-3 if n=='RH' else 3,4))
   parts[n]={'lower':lower,'knee':knee}
  used |= rect(80,140,84,149)|rect(107,140,111,149)
 body=b.piece(ref,~used)
 if v=='s':
  # Keep the native full chest, all shading and seams, including its taper.
  body[Y>=147]=0
  body[rect(80,140,89,146)&~np.logical_or.reduce([np.all(ref[:,:,:3]==list(bytes.fromhex(h)),axis=2) for h in ['fff4d6','f0d27a','b8923a']])]=0
  body[rect(102,140,111,146)&~np.logical_or.reduce([np.all(ref[:,:,:3]==list(bytes.fromhex(h)),axis=2) for h in ['fff4d6','f0d27a','b8923a']])]=0
 else:
  body[Y>=129]=0
 for n,pp in parts.items():
  q=D[v][n];q['sole']=b.support(pp['lower']).tolist()
  for p,a in pp.items():save(a,f'source/{v}_{n}_{p}.png')
 save(body,f'source/{v}_body.png');save(tail,f'source/{v}_tail.png')
 return ref,parts,body,tail

def band(a,p0,p1,width=5,far=False):
 old.band(a,p0,p1,width,far)

def make(v):
 ref,parts,body,tail=sources(v);frames=[];rig=[]
 for f in range(8):
  dip=int(f in [0,4]);ll={}
  for n,pp in parts.items():
   q=D[v][n];sole=np.array(q['sole']);target=np.array(T['views'][v]['visible_feet'][n]['frames'][f]['sole_centre'])
   delta=np.floor(target-sole+.5).astype(int);dx,dy=map(int,delta);kx=int(np.floor(dx*.5+.5));ky=int(np.floor((dy+dip)*.5+.5))
   root=np.array(q['root'])+[0,dip];kn=np.array(q['k'])+[kx,ky];jn=np.array(q['join'])+delta
   a=np.zeros_like(ref);far=n.endswith('H') if v=='s' else n.endswith('F')

def cmd_seams(a):
    t = json.load(open(a.targets))
    body = {tuple(bytes.fromhex(c)) for c in a.body} if a.body else BODY_GREENS
    if a.fix:
        os.makedirs(a.fix, exist_ok=True)
    for view in (a.views or list(t['views'])):
        st = load_rgba(os.path.join(a.still_dir, f'{view}.png'))
        counts = []
        for i in range(8):
            name = a.pattern.format(view=view, i=i)
            fr = load_rgba(os.path.join(a.frames, name))
            dip = 1 if i in t['dip_frames'] else 0
            sl = find_slits(fr, st, dip)
            counts.append(len(sl))
            if a.fix:
                done = set()
                for x, y in sorted(sl, key=lambda q: (q[1], q[0])):
                    fr[y, x, :3] = fill_colour(fr, st, dip, x, y, done, body)
                    fr[y, x, 3] = 255
                    done.add((x, y))
                Image.fromarray(fr).save(os.path.join(a.fix, name))
        print(f'{view}: enclosed slit px per frame {counts}' + (' (filled)' if a.fix else ''))
    print('Look at every slit before filling: a real gap between a limb and the body must stay open.')


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- mirror
def cmd_mirror(a):
    gait = GAITS[a.gait]
    up = [sorted(f for f, s in gait.items() if (i - s) % 8 < 3) for i in range(8)]
    print(f'gait {a.gait}: feet up per frame in the drawn views (s, se, e, ne, n)')
    print('frame | drawn up | mirrored view, same frame (physical) | mirrored view at frame+4 (physical) | same?')
    same4 = True
    for i in range(8):
        m0 = sorted(swap_lr(f) for f in up[i])
        m4 = sorted(swap_lr(f) for f in up[(i + 4) % 8])
        same4 &= (m4 == up[i])
        print(f'  f{i}  | {",".join(up[i]) or "-":8} | {",".join(m0) or "-":36} | {",".join(m4) or "-":36} | '
              f'{"yes" if m4 == up[i] else "no"}')
    print('A turn from s to sw (or n to nw) keeps the frame index today, so the up foot swaps sides in one frame.')
    print('Offset +4 keeps the same physical foot up: ' + ('yes, for every frame' if same4 else 'NO, check the gait'))


def main():
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    sub = p.add_subparsers(dest='cmd', required=True)
    s = sub.add_parser('stride')
    s.add_argument('--size', choices=TEXEL_M, required=True)
    s.add_argument('--leg-px', type=float, nargs='+', required=True, help='hip or shoulder height above the feet row')
    s.add_argument('--P', type=float, default=3)
    s.add_argument('--fps', type=float, default=8)
    s.add_argument('--move-speed', type=float, default=3.5)
    s = sub.add_parser('soles')
    s.add_argument('images', nargs='+')
    s.add_argument('--offset', type=int, nargs=2, default=[0, 0])
    s.add_argument('--canvas', type=int, default=0)
    s.add_argument('--depth', type=int, default=24, help='list soles up to this many rows over the lowest row')
    s = sub.add_parser('gait')
    s.add_argument('--feet', required=True)
    s.add_argument('--views-dir', required=True)
    s.add_argument('--gait', choices=GAITS, required=True)
    s.add_argument('--P', type=float, default=3)  # float: the fennec trots at 3.5 px a frame
    s.add_argument('--fps', type=int, default=8)
    s.add_argument('--size', choices=TEXEL_M, required=True)
    s.add_argument('--lifts', default='2,3,2')
    s.add_argument('--pitch', type=float, default=35)
    s.add_argument('--scale', type=int, default=3)
    s.add_argument('--out', required=True)
    s = sub.add_parser('footlock')
    s.add_argument('--targets', required=True)
    s.add_argument('--frames', required=True)
    s.add_argument('--pattern', default='{view}_f{i}.png')
    s.add_argument('--views', nargs='*')
    s.add_argument('--still-dir')
    s.add_argument('--still-offset', type=int, nargs=2, default=[0, 0])
    s.add_argument('--out')
    s = sub.add_parser('seams')
    s.add_argument('--targets', required=True, help='for the views and dip frames')
    s.add_argument('--frames', required=True)
    s.add_argument('--still-dir', required=True, help='stills already on the frame canvas')
    s.add_argument('--pattern', default='{view}_f{i}.png')
    s.add_argument('--views', nargs='*')
    s.add_argument('--body', nargs='*', help='hex colours a fill may use (default: the gorilla greens)')
    s.add_argument('--fix', help='write filled frames to this folder (never over the input)')
    s = sub.add_parser('mirror')
    s.add_argument('--gait', choices=GAITS, required=True)
    a = p.parse_args()
    {'stride': cmd_stride, 'soles': cmd_soles, 'gait': cmd_gait, 'footlock': cmd_footlock, 'seams': cmd_seams,
     'mirror': cmd_mirror}[a.cmd](a)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
